# Decisions (ADR-lite; one line each: what + why)

- 2026-06-12 · Tauri 2 over Electron/SwiftUI: small/fast/native feel; Rust core for streaming perf.
- 2026-06-12 · tokio-postgres over sqlx: need RowDescription `table_oid()`/`column_id()` for editable results, CancelToken, notices.
- 2026-06-12 · CodeMirror 6 over Monaco: ~1MB vs ~5MB, faster in WKWebView; intellisense quality comes from our completion engine either way.
- 2026-06-12 · Custom DOM grid (TanStack Virtual) over Glide Data Grid: full UX control; Glide is the fallback at P2 checkpoint.
- 2026-06-12 · SSH tunnels via system `ssh -L` subprocess: respects ~/.ssh/config; russh only if subprocess proves flaky.
- 2026-06-12 · Passwords in macOS Keychain (keyring crate); rusqlite app-db stores everything else.
- 2026-06-12 · JSON row values over Channel v1; binary/Arrow only if profiling demands.
- 2026-06-12 · zustand for state; `motion` for springs; lucide icons; cmdk palette.
- 2026-06-12 · `time` pinned to 0.3.47 in Cargo.lock: 0.3.48 breaks tauri-utils 2.9.2 with E0119 (conflicting From impls). Unpin when tauri-utils ships a fix; don't `cargo update` blindly.
- 2026-06-12 · keyring crate pinned to v3 (apple-native): v4 is a restructured meta-crate needing store init; v3 API is stable and sufficient.
- 2026-06-12 · Execution uses PG **simple protocol** (`simple_query`): every type arrives as wire text (psql-identical), multi-statement native. P5 editability metadata comes from a separate `prepare()` (RowDescription table_oid/attnum) without re-executing.
- 2026-06-12 · TLS = rustls with no-verify ServerCertVerifier ≈ psql `sslmode=require` semantics (RDS works out of the box); `prefer` tries TLS then falls back to plain.

### v0.1.5
- 2026-06-13 · Editability via `prepare()` only (no re-exec); ctid is the row-locator fallback when no PK is in the result (editable-with-warning); multi-cell edits to one row batch into a single UPDATE (`plan_edits` groups by table+row).
- 2026-06-13 · One dedicated PG connection per query tab (`ensureTabSession`) so transactions/temp state stay coherent; schema introspection runs on a separate per-profile *primary* session. Tx-open indicator is a SQL-sniff heuristic (tokio-postgres doesn't expose ReadyForQuery).
- 2026-06-13 · `window.confirm()` is a silent no-op in WKWebView → all destructive actions use a custom DangerModal or a two-click arm pattern, never `confirm()`.
- 2026-06-13 · WKWebView forces macOS autocorrect/capitalize on every field with no global switch → `app/noAutocorrect.ts` stamps it off per-field + a MutationObserver for new fields (opt-out `data-allow-autocorrect`).
- 2026-06-14 · SSH tunnel = system `ssh -N -L` subprocess, one shared per profile (`AppState.tunnels`), `BatchMode=yes`, honours `~/.ssh/config`; routes connect through the local port. `kill_on_drop` reaps it.

### v0.2
- 2026-06-14 · Theme engine (`design/theme.ts`): a palette is *seeds*, expanded to the full CSS-var token set as inline vars at startup (no flash). Two kinds: **hue** (curated: accent+hue+tint → tinted neutral ramp) and **anchors** (custom: bg/fg/primary/secondary → surfaces by sRGB mix). `--accent-fg` is auto-contrast. Custom themes are authored once; the opposite light/dark variant is synthesised so the mode toggle flips them too.
- 2026-06-14 · Floating-card shell on a vibrancy gutter: transparent window + `window-vibrancy` material shows through `--gutter` between `.card` panels (rail / sidebar / main / inspector). Replaced the old flush `.app-shell/.sidebar/.main-area`.
- 2026-06-14 · Per-tab results+edits keyed `byTab` with the active tab mirrored to top-level store fields → every consumer reads unchanged and a background tab's stream can't corrupt the visible tab. `committing`/`preview` stay global (one commit at a time).
- 2026-06-14 · Connection liveness: `connect` takes an `on_close` callback the driver task fires on socket death → `session-closed` event → frontend flips the dot + drops dead sessions; `ensureTabSession` auto-reconnects on next use.
- 2026-06-15 · Editing a saved connection invalidates its live state when connection-affecting fields change (`connSig`): close the profile's sessions + drop its cached tunnel so the next connect uses the new values. The tunnel also carries a `spec` (host+ssh params) and `ensure_tunnel` rebuilds on mismatch; a repointed host can't keep forwarding to the old one. Cosmetic edits leave the live connection alone.
- 2026-06-15 · Inspector commit/preview re-resolve a live session via `ensureTabSession` instead of the result's `executedSessionId` (which can be dead after a repoint/drop/dev-rebuild); edits are pk-based so they apply on a fresh connection.
- 2026-06-15 · Colorized JSON view/edit uses a CodeMirror `JsonField`, never a transparent-textarea-over-`<pre>` overlay; WKWebView's opaque native field background always covers the colored layer.
- 2026-06-15 · App icon built from the transparent SVG via `scripts/make_icon.py` → `icon-master.png` composited onto Apple's grid (824² rounded squircle in 1024, charcoal gradient, soft shadow); all sizes regenerated by `tauri icon`.

### v0.2.5
- 2026-06-17 · Tabs unified: `Tab` gains `kind: "query"|"table"` + `table` ref; opening a table creates a **session-only** table tab (persist only query tabs, stripped to the appdb fields) instead of a full-panel swap. App renders editor vs `TableBrowser` by the active tab's kind; tab strip shows a kind icon.
- 2026-06-17 · Browser state went **per-tab**: `useBrowser` keyed `byTab` mirrored from the active tab (results/edits pattern); the table ref lives on the `Tab`, so every table tab keeps its own filters/sort/scroll/draft. Opening an already-open table focuses it (no dupes).
- 2026-06-17 · Column-header data-type icons come from the **editability map's `type_name`** (already fetched eagerly per result); the simple-protocol `ColumnMeta` carries no type, so no Rust change. `grid/typeIcon.tsx` maps type families → lucide glyph + `--syn-*` color; reuses `isArrayType`.
- 2026-06-17 · Inline add-row replaces the `InsertPanel` overlay: a sticky draft band pinned under the grid header (outside the virtualizer; cells offset `+draftH`), per-tab `draftRow` in the browser store; `⌘↵` insert / `Esc` cancel / `∅` NULL. Add-row button moved into the bottom toolbar (far left; Filter+Sort right).
- 2026-06-17 · Close-with-unsaved-edits guard: `closeGuard` store routes every close (tab X / ⌘W / browser header X) → prompts when `useEdits.byTab[id].pending` is non-empty. `Esc` keep · `Enter` discard&close · `⌘↵` commit&close (selects the tab first so the commit targets it; keeps the tab open if commit fails).
- 2026-06-17 · Connect errors surfaced: TLS→plain fallback no longer masks **server-sent** errors (`e.as_db_error()`); a bad password / pg_hba now reports its real reason instead of a misleading "no encryption". Global `ConnToast` shows connect failures on any view (dashboard included) with an Edit shortcut.
- 2026-06-17 · Tab strip auto-scrolls the active tab into view via `scrollIntoView({ inline: "nearest" })`; minimal slide, no jitter when already visible; reveals the `+` for the last tab. Native horizontal scrollbar hidden.

### v0.3 → v0.5 (releasable push)
- 2026-06-26 · The v0.3→v0.5 plan lives in `docs/ROADMAP_v0.5.md` (active plan doc); main `ROADMAP.md` keeps the v0.1–v0.2.5 history + session log + a pointer banner. Drafted from a 16-workstream audit + competitor-research workflow (8 subsystem code-audits + TablePlus/Postico/Beekeeper/DataGrip/DBeaver/community inventories → gap matrix → completeness critic → roadmap).
- 2026-06-26 · v0.3 builds a **shared overlay primitive** FIRST (one z-index / Esc / outside-click / viewport-clamp layer for every popup), then a nested `ContextMenu`/`Submenu` on top: root-fix for the off-screen CellEditor + the no-Esc ThemePicker + the 3 hand-rolled flat menus, and the substrate for the right-click-everywhere epic. Don't hand-roll another popup.
- 2026-06-26 · Find = one mental model: ⌘F scoped to the focused surface (editor via `@codemirror/search`; results over the in-memory rows with an honest "N of M loaded / capped" indicator; JsonTree already has ⌘F). Esc closes, ⏎/⇧⏎ next/prev, match count shown; identical chrome everywhere.
- 2026-06-26 · Perf is gated by **measurement, not feel**: v0.4 records before/after numbers for cold-start / keystroke→completion / 50k scroll / drag-select against the stated budgets in the session note; a milestone Gate isn't green until its perf slice is measured.

### Mission upgrade (2026-07-02)
- 2026-07-02 · Goal changed from "releasable v0.5" (endpoint) to **surpass every mainstream Postgres client**: no finish line; v0.5 stays a waypoint/version cap for the current push. Two invariants outrank all feature work: (1) never feels slow at any data scale, (2) never lies about or corrupts data. Feature count only grows.
- 2026-07-02 · `docs/GAPS.md` is the ranked gap census (from a 13-workstream code+competitor audit; raw reports in `docs/audit-2026-07/`). Roadmap milestones draw from it: correctness ledger (GAPS §1) before features; the correctness keystone is **statement-at-a-time execution** in the driver (replaces whole-buffer `simple_query`); prerequisite for matched≠1 rollback, honest multi-statement results, tx-state tracking.
- 2026-07-02 · Promotions locked: run-statement-under-cursor and selection-stats-in-status-bar are P0 (v0.4); v0.35 = Correctness Milestone. Leapfrog features (GAPS §4: run-CTE-standalone, reverse-FK lookup, inverse-SQL undo, impact estimates…) are the beyond-v0.5 identity; build those before parity-polish.
- 2026-07-02 · **Multi-statement runs = psql autocommit semantics** (statement-at-a-time `simple_query`, stop on first error, earlier statements stay committed), NOT an atomic implicit transaction (Postico's model). Rationale: the whole-buffer form made the UI lie (reported-committed work silently rolled back by a later error) and broke VACUUM/CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY. Explicit BEGIN/COMMIT still span statements on the tab's session. A "run script atomically" toggle can layer on later. Error positions are rebased to whole-buffer CHAR offsets in the driver (`StmtSpan.char_offset`).
- 2026-07-02 · **Session timeouts: statement_timeout=5min + idle_in_transaction=10min at connect.** Deviates from locked decision #6 (30s): without a Settings escape hatch, 30s would kill legitimate analytics queries on big staging tables daily. 5min is the interim never-hangs-forever net (keepalives 30s/10s×3 handle the dead-tunnel case in ~1min); revisit the number when Settings (⌘,) lands.
- 2026-07-02 · **ROW_CAP auto-cancel is best-effort by design**: the driver cancels its own drain when the cap is crossed on the last statement, but a fast query legitimately wins the race and completes as a normal capped drain. Both paths return Ok + capped; a 57014 is only swallowed when WE fired the cancel (user ⌘. still errors through).
- **Prod safe-mode is server-side, per-session** (2026-07-03): `is_prod` → `default_transaction_read_only=on` in connect options (every session incl. spares), unlock = `SET … = off` on ONE tab's session behind a confirm. Chosen over client-side SQL sniffing: the server can't be fooled by CTEs/DO blocks/functions. Caveat: PG read-only still permits writes to existing TEMP tables; acceptable (no persistent data at risk).
- **Statement timeout is an app-level setting applied at connect** (2026-07-03): stored in useSettings (300s default, 0 = none), passed per `ipc.connect`; existing sessions keep their timeout until rebuilt. Per-profile override deferred until someone needs it.
- **⌘⇧F = format in the editor, filter-focus elsewhere** (2026-07-03): CodeMirror keymap preventDefaults first, window listener bails on defaultPrevented; one chord, two context-correct meanings.
- **Active tab restore via localStorage, not appdb** (2026-07-03): UI nicety, not data: losing it is harmless, so no migration/schema cost.
- **Per-connection workspaces** (2026-07-06): tabs carry `profile_id`; visibility = own ∪ pinned ∪ legacy-null. Pins are the cross-connection mechanism (origin-colored badge). Legacy tabs adopt-on-edit rather than bulk-migrate (no wrong guesses, no disappearing tabs). Unpin with a dead/foreign origin adopts the current connection. DB-switcher clones get fresh workspaces (different profile id); deliberate: different database, different tabs.
- **Commits are ONE batched simple-query message** (2026-07-06): `BEGIN;U₁;…;Uₙ` in one round trip, per-statement RETURNING counts verified from the single result stream, COMMIT/ROLLBACK as trip two; 2 RTTs for any N. Result-set accounting must be exact (n+1 sets) or the batch is refused+rolled back; any error mid-batch rolls back everything. Verify-then-commit semantics unchanged, only the transport shape.
- **Edit mappings are frontend-fed, trusted-but-verified** (2026-07-06): preview/apply/delete accept the EditabilityMap+snapshot-names the frontend already holds (zero catalog trips; preview = zero server contact), and `editability` accepts snapshot identity hints (1 RTT). Safety: incomplete hint → silent server derivation; snapshot table must match the map's server-derived dotted name (cross-DB oid collision guard); stale hints can only end in rollback (SQL error or matched≠1). SQLSTATE class-42 → refetch map; preview retries once silently, apply NEVER auto-retries; it regenerates the preview with a "schema changed" notice. Commit replays the exact stashed preview inputs (byte-for-byte guarantee).
- **Schema snapshots persist per profile (stale-while-revalidate)** (2026-07-06): appdb `schema_cache` keyed profile_id + `connSig`; hydrate at connect START for t=0 sidebar/completion, real introspect refreshes behind it. Ordering rule: hydrate never overwrites anything, server fetch always wins; no timestamps needed. Raw-JSON introspect passthrough rejected (escaping bloat + loses Rust-side validation).
- **SSH tunnels keyed and shared by SPEC, not profile** (2026-07-06): DB-switcher clones ride one ssh process. `profile_specs` tracks bindings; invalidate/delete unbinds the profile and drops the tunnel only when no other profile shares the spec (a shared tunnel drop would kill sibling sessions). A repointed profile computes a new spec and naturally gets a fresh tunnel.

### v0.7.0-bedrock
- 2026-07-18 · appdb schema versioned via `PRAGMA user_version` + numbered migrations, one transaction each, stamped on commit (replaces the `let _ = ALTER` swallow-all). v1 = pre-versioning baseline: CREATE IF NOT EXISTS + column-checked ALTERs, so a legacy unversioned DB is stamped in place with data intact and real errors propagate. List reads (profiles/tabs/saved/history) skip-and-log corrupt rows: one bad row costs one row, never the whole list. History rows carry status ok/error/cancelled (failed + ⌘.-cancelled runs are recorded from the frontend catch path, 57014 = cancelled), per-row SQL capped at 20k chars with a marker, table pruned to newest 20k rows on insert. `tauri-plugin-single-instance` registered before every other plugin; a second launch focuses the existing window instead of racing the first for the shared appdb.
- 2026-07-18 · **Tx state is driver-lexed** (tokio-postgres discards ReadyForQuery's status byte at every site; checked 0.7.17 source): splitter statement-heads (BEGIN/START, COMMIT/END, ROLLBACK/ABORT, ROLLBACK TO un-fails, AND CHAIN stays in-tx, PREPARE TRANSACTION → idle) + error outcomes fold into idle/in-tx/failed-tx per session, surfaced as a dedicated `tx-state` event (not a QueryEvent). Known limit: `CALL proc()` with internal COMMIT drifts the tracker, but only in the fail-safe direction (worst case a SAVEPOINT errors; nothing can be silently committed). Ride-along: ROW_CAP auto-cancel is disabled inside an open tx (our own 57014 would abort the user's transaction).
- 2026-07-18 · **Edits inside a user transaction = SAVEPOINT/RELEASE** (`ROLLBACK TO`+`RELEASE` on verify failure); the outer tx is NEVER committed or rolled back by an edit. `committed:true` there means "applied into the open tx"; durability is the user's COMMIT (tx chip stays lit). Batch wrapper contributes exactly one result set in both modes, keeping n+1 stream accounting.
- 2026-07-18 · **ctid guards use `= 'v'::cast` / `IS NULL`, not IS NOT DISTINCT FROM**: semantically identical for known old values, and stays indexable; json/xml/geometric types (no `=` operator) compare via `::text`. Guard columns: every untruncated same-table result column, deduped by attnum. Same guard grammar on edit and delete paths.
- 2026-07-18 · **Table identity is `{schema, name}` end-to-end** (`table_refs` in EditabilityMap/EditMapHint, schema+name on TableIdentityHint); the dotted `tables` string survives as display-only. Casts are captured pre-qualified at editability time (`pg_catalog` bare, else quoted+qualified; `"char"` force-quoted: bare `::char` silently means bpchar(1)).
- 2026-07-18 · **Cancel escalates out-of-band**: CancelToken (1.5s deadline) → busy-poll ≤1.2s → `pg_cancel_backend` over a fresh connection built from the stored Config (5s deadline); `pg_terminate_backend` is a separate user-confirmed command on the force-disconnect hatch, never automatic. Internal ROW_CAP cancel uses the raw token (escalation would self-deadlock on the busy poll).
- 2026-07-18 · **Overlay z-index derives from escStack position** (BASE 50 + depth via `pushOverlay`); stacking order can't lie about key routing. escStack ignores Esc while `e.isComposing`.
- 2026-07-18 · **Draft-row grammar**: untouched = DEFAULT, touched-empty = `''`, ∅ = NULL; touched-state per cell. Revised same day after audit: an EMPTY field in a multi-cell TSV spread-paste stays untouched (DEFAULT, the spreadsheet convention; `''` into numeric/date columns was hostile); a deliberate `''` remains reachable per cell (type + clear, or single-cell paste).
- 2026-07-18 · **Hint-fed plans carry attname identity guards** (audit fix): every hint-named column used in a hinted statement (SET + locator + guard) gets `(SELECT attname FROM pg_attribute WHERE attrelid=… AND attnum=…) = 'name'` ANDed into the WHERE; an external RENAME+ADD between snapshot and ⌘S now rolls back honestly (matched 0) instead of writing the wrong column. Derived (non-hint) plans carry none; still 2 RTTs.
- 2026-07-18 · **Savepoint hygiene** (audit fix): per-batch unique savepoint names (`qwry_edit_sp_{n}`) so our cleanup can never roll back a user's identically-named savepoint; batches REFUSE to start in a failed tx ("current transaction is aborted; ROLLBACK first"). Failed COMMIT/END (deferred constraints) folds Idle, matching the server.
- 2026-07-19 · **Sort is a chain** (spelunk): `setSortChain(SortKey[])` ({column, dir, nulls?}) is the browse sort API; per-key NULLS FIRST/LAST are keyset ladder VARIANTS (default placements emit byte-identical SQL to before, proven); tiebreaker inherits the FIRST key's direction, PK-in-chain absorbed. Supersedes "no NULLS clauses" phrasing in the 07-18 keyset entry. Editor results: stable client-side multi-sort in the existing view-map slot.
- 2026-07-19 · **Raw-WHERE mode owns the WHERE entirely** (spelunk): builder rows can never filter behind the user's back while raw mode is on; one `compiledWhere` feeds pages, count, and histograms. Jump-to-row = honest OFFSET page-1, then scrolling re-anchors keyset from the landed page (proven gapless).
- 2026-07-19 · Sidebar pins persist in localStorage per profile as {schema,name} pairs (UI nicety, active-tab-restore precedent). Introspect v2 requires PG 10+ (pg_sequence join); pre-10 errors the whole batch; ledgered, PG 9.6 is EOL.
- 2026-07-19 · **ctid keyset is triple-gated** (tempo audit): ordinary relkind only, `relhassubclass=false` (inheritance children have colliding ctids: TimescaleDB hypertables), AND reltuples estimate ≤1M (TID scans have no pathkeys; ORDER BY ctid is a seq scan + top-N sort PER PAGE); missing metadata fails safe to offset fallback. Page-1 keys are PINNED on the tab: key drift (snapshot refresh) or a committed edit to a key column forces a full re-run, never a spliced append. Inherent residual (ledgered): concurrent writers move ctids between pages.
- 2026-07-18 · **Browse pagination is true keyset** (tempo): PK/ctid tiebreaker inherits the sort direction (uniform ORDER BY, page 1 included; total order requires it); row-value `(a,b)>(v…)` fast path only when uniform-dir AND catalog NOT NULL; otherwise OR-ladder with explicit NULL partitions; sort keys always treated nullable (stale snapshots); seek values are wire text + `::format_type` casts. Offset fallback ONLY for non-keysettable relations (views, partitioned-no-PK, ctid on PG<14, truncated seek cells), never wrong-rows keyset. loadMore appends one page on the tab session and skips history.
- 2026-07-18 · **pg_catalog functions cached in appdb keyed by server_version** (tempo): introspect stays one round trip: a count(*) probe rides the batch; count drift (extension into pg_catalog) or corrupt JSON = miss → refetch+re-persist. User-schema functions always live.
- 2026-07-18 · **Editor statement spans are incremental** (tempo): prefix reuse + 64KB growing re-lex window + suffix re-adopt on exact shifted restart; the FINAL span is always re-lexed (a trailing `;` can sit inside an unterminated comment; differential testing caught this). Completion context is capped at a 200KB window around the cursor with byte-identical output under the cap; sql-sync deliberately keeps one synchronous toString() per keystroke (0.016ms JSC rope) because ⌘S/close/quit read the store synchronously.
- 2026-07-18 · `history_search` LIKE-scans only the newest 5k rows (constant cost; FTS is the lift if older-match search is ever wanted).
- 2026-07-18 · `ConnState` gains `"error"` (failed connect ≠ never-tried gray); every existing consumer compares `=== "connected"` so it behaves as not-connected everywhere else.

### v0.8.1-timeline
- 2026-07-19 · **Undo OLD-capture is in-statement, zero extra RTTs**: `UPDATE t AS __t SET … FROM (SELECT <changed> FROM t WHERE <full-where> FOR UPDATE) __old WHERE <full-where on __t> RETURNING __old.*, __t.*, __t.<locator>`; the outer WHERE repeats the predicates (indexable, matched-count byte-equivalent; FOR UPDATE locks before capture so __old is the exact pre-image). PG 14–17 grammar; PG 18 OLD/NEW RETURNING unused. DELETE captures via RETURNING * + one catalog probe riding the batch (DDL blocked by the DELETEs' own lock).
- 2026-07-19 · **The undo artifact is a structured revert PLAN, not SQL text**: SQL regenerated by the same generator at undo time (nothing parsed); an applied undo mints its own inverse, so redo emerges with zero special-case code. Reverts: PK locators (post-commit values), NEW ctid + server-normalized value guards on ctid tables, attname identity guards always; GENERATED/identity-ALWAYS excluded from re-INSERTs (no OVERRIDING SYSTEM VALUE: honesty over magic; an undone delete of an identity-ALWAYS row gets a fresh id, ledgered).
- 2026-07-19 · **Undo re-enters run_verified_batch**: stale undo = matched≠1 = full rollback + "undo no longer matches". Offer is session-stamped (server re-checked), single-shot (consumed whether it commits or not, NEVER auto-retried), TTL 15min, invalidated on DDL/newer-commit/session-death; prod read-only refuses at the server (tested).
- 2026-07-19 · **Buffer time-machine views are read-only throwaway EditorStates**: the live draft is parked and every store-sync path is hard-gated while viewing, so a snapshot can never leak into tab.sql; restore = one undoable transaction. Snapshots ride appdb (v5) keyed per tab, deduped, capped 50.
- 2026-07-19 · .sql file links are session-only Tab fields (path + saved-text); persisting them needs a tabs schema column; deferred. Drag-drop uses Tauri window events (WKWebView never delivers HTML5 file drops with dragDropEnabled).
- 2026-07-19 · **CSV import: validate = a full rehearsal that ALWAYS rolls back; commit = ONE all-or-nothing tx** (timeline): both run identical batched 500-row multi-row INSERTs with `'v'::type` casts (cast names from live pg_attribute/pg_type, edit.rs cast discipline). Validate re-runs a failed batch row-by-row under savepoints to name exact bad rows (cap 50 + honest "more"); commit failure attributes the row via PG's char-position→tuple-span map, else names the batch range. `flexible(false)`: short/long rows are per-row errors, never padded. Header sniff rule: row 1 is a header ONLY on a type mismatch vs the data rows (all-match ⇒ headerless; never eat a data row); UTF-8 only, per-row errors otherwise. Fatal codes (25006 read-only prod, 25P02, 57014, 08*) abort instead of repeating.
- 2026-07-19 · import.rs carries byte-identical private copies of edit.rs `ql`/`qi`/cast rules with parity unit tests; edit.rs was concurrently owned in this wave so the helpers couldn't be made pub(crate); fold them into one shared module next quiet pass.
- 2026-07-19 · History panel: grouping is CLIENT-side over the unchanged history_search API: consecutive same-normalized-SQL+same-profile runs collapse to ×N (expand →/←, badge tints when a collapsed group hides non-ok runs); day dividers Today/Yesterday/date; per-connection chip pushes profile_id into the query, status chips filter client-side; absolute timestamp on hover, relative stays primary.

### v0.9.0-flightcheck
- 2026-07-19 · **Destructive-confirm grammar is ⌘⌫, Enter = safe** (Postico convention): CloseGuard/DangerModal map plain Enter to Keep/Cancel and the destructive confirm to ⌘⌫ (rendered on the button); ⌘↵ stays commit-&-close on CloseGuard. EditPreview's Enter=Commit is DELIBERATE (primary action of an explicitly opened review, not a destructive surprise; documented in-file). DangerModal's old ⌘↵ confirm is gone.
- 2026-07-19 · **Overlay focus discipline lives in the primitive**: escStack captures the opener per layer (at render, before autoFocus steals focus) and restores on pop via microtask; skipped when a newer overlay opened (owns focus) or the close deliberately focused something (activeElement ≠ body); fallback = main editor → .main-card (tabIndex -1), never <body>. Modal traps Tab/⇧Tab topmost-only + role=dialog/aria-modal/aria-label from every consumer, and pulls focus off the background at open (typing can't leak into the editor behind a dialog). Menus/popovers stay focusless by design (roles only).
- 2026-07-19 · **UI zoom is chrome-level** (⌘= / ⌘− / ⌘0, 70–150%, persisted): the px --text-* tokens are re-emitted as inline overrides scaled by uiZoom (bases read once from the loaded stylesheet, so tokens.css stays authoritative) + root font-size % + a --zoom factor for opt-in CSS. Editor (--editor-fs) and grid (--grid-fs + row geometry) are EXCLUDED: they have their own size settings and the grid's virtualizer/hit-test math is px-anchored. ⌘0 moved from jump-to-tab-10 to zoom reset (tabs are ⌘1…9); menu accelerator is Cmd+= (muda has no "Plus" key).

### v0.9.1-firstlight
- 2026-07-20 · **Hidden-until-painted launch**: the window is created `visible: false` and revealed by the frontend after startup data (profiles+tabs) settles + double-rAF post-paint; never an empty glass pane. window-state plugin runs with `StateFlags::all() - VISIBLE` (restoring visibility would defeat the hidden start); a Rust 5s fallback thread shows the window if the webview never boots. Corollary: any future "show earlier" change must keep all three pieces in sync.
- 2026-07-20 · **Store wiring pins the entry chunk**: stores with module-level cross-store `subscribe()` (edits, browser) are side-effect-imported in main.tsx. Lazy-chunking them starts their wiring late and desyncs active-tab state; same failure class as the HMR stale-store gotcha. Shell reads editor registries via `editor/editorBus.ts`, never from SqlEditor (which would drag CodeMirror into the entry chunk).
- 2026-07-20 · **One-line inputs never hold newlines**: draft cells with multiline text render a read-only ⏎ preview and edit through ValuePop (WebKit silently strips \n from input.value → state desync). Draft paste inserts at the CARET; merged-valid-JSON compacts; everything else goes to the pop-out; a paste never replaces cell state wholesale. ValuePop `startDirty` exists because its no-change close would otherwise swallow paste-opened content.
- 2026-07-20 · **Draft JSON compaction is display-only and guarded**: parse validates, but compaction (json/jsonb columns ONLY, never text-like) is skipped when the text trips LOSSY_NUMS: >2^53 ints don't survive a JS parse/stringify round-trip; the exact typed bytes stage instead (⏎ preview absorbs the multiline). Audit caught both draft paths skipping the guard the inspector already enforces. Companion: `startup_ok` IPC. The refused-appdb path manages a fatal stub state and the frontend reveal checks it, so the hidden window never pops up empty beside the "qwry can't start" dialog.

### v0.9.2-lookingglass
- 2026-07-20 · **Translucent tokens never carry sticky-overlay duty alone**: `--accent-soft` (and any alpha token) must be layered over an opaque base (`linear-gradient(tint, tint), var(--bg-*)`) when the element occludes scrolling content (the draft-corner gutter bug). Layout probes (elementFromPoint/rects) cannot see this class; repro harnesses need pixel evidence.
- 2026-07-20 · **Programmatic focus in the grid always uses `preventScroll: true`** and scroll authority belongs to `scrollToIndex` alone; WKWebView's focus-reveal scrolls to the element's FLOW (unstuck) position and had yanked the scroller to origin via draft-cell `autoFocus` on remount. Focus-once refs replace autoFocus in virtualized bands; remount-refocus only when `document.activeElement === document.body`.
- 2026-07-20 · **Virtualizers under sticky chrome get `scrollPaddingEnd`** (= the chrome's px) or end-aligned scrolls park the target under/behind the chrome; TanStack v3 re-reads options per render so a variable band height is safe.
- 2026-07-20 · **Grid z ladder is documented order, not ad-hoc**: celledit 5 < rownums 6 < header 7 < draft band 8 < corner/dropline 9 < draft-error 10; overlays 20/30; toast 45; escStack 50–59; prod strip 60. New grid chrome slots into this ladder explicitly.
- 2026-07-20 · **Store actions capture their tab at entry, never after an await**: commitDraft/insertRow now thread the captured tabId and skip re-runs when the active tab changed (loadMore's rule, made universal). Post-await `get().active` is the recurring wrong-tab-write bug class.
- 2026-07-20 · **All clipboard writes route through `lib/copyCue.ts`** (toast cue, failure-visible). Bare writeText in UI code is a defect; the cue toast lives at z 45 (below overlays: feedback, never interaction).
- 2026-07-20 · **⌘⇧F = Format SQL everywhere** (editor keymap + global fallback via editorBus); schema-filter focus is ⌥⌘F matched by `e.code === "KeyF"` (⌥F produces "ƒ"; e.key comparisons break under ⌥).
- 2026-07-20 · **TSV paste parses the quoting convention back** (`parseTsv` in clipboard.ts, exact inverse of the writer, null on malformed → naive-split fallback): both paste surfaces (grid ⌘V, draft band) were naive-splitting, so a copied json cell landed as `"[""x""]"` and a quoted newline split one cell into two rows. Boundary: single-line pastes (no tab/newline) NEVER unquote: a pasted JSON string literal must stay verbatim; unquoting only where TSV structure is unambiguous.
- 2026-07-21 · **Draft-row value grammar: empty input = DEFAULT, always.** DraftCell {text, state?: "null"|"empty"}; the old touched-but-empty='' convention committed '' when a pasted PK was ⌫-cleared. Explicit NULL/'' are deliberate acts (per-cell menu / ⌥⌫-on-empty; with text present ⌥⌫ stays macOS word-delete), rendered as the grid's own chips; ∅ button retired. Placeholders self-document from the schema snapshot (default expr / "identity" / "required" / NULL); identity-ALWAYS + generated render "auto" and are never staged (dup-row skips them too). The required pre-check resolves columns from the LIVE snapshot first; refusing a legal write off a tab-frozen TableInfo is the "app lies" failure mode. "Set empty" is anchored to scalar text-family types ('' into text[] = malformed array literal).

### v0.9.5-truename
- 2026-07-21 · **Workspace identity rules** (from the cross-conn table-open bug): (1) any global tab lookup that ends in select() MUST scope to the visible workspace (own ∪ legacy-null ∪ pinned); unscoped matches select foreign tabs whose cached rows render under the wrong connection's branding; select() itself now refuses invisible ids (dev-warn). (2) **Never write to A and repaint from B**: post-write reloads (insert, import, delete, undo) re-run through the profile that executed the write via run()'s new {profileId} override; every other run keeps v0.6 rail semantics ("pinned tabs run against the ACTIVE conn"). (3) The chrome must speak for the ROWS, not just the rail: origin provenance banner when executedProfileId ≠ rail; prod strip + write-lock chip key on the origin (a prod-pinned tab under a staging rail was rendering prod rows + committing prod writes with zero prod ceremony); EditPreview names its commit target; tx surfaces scan ALL of a tab's sessions (skey suffix), rollback targets the session holding the tx.
- 2026-07-22 · **One row-view concept, one gesture**: RowPeek deleted; Space, ⇧Space, and `Open as Record` all open Record View (strict superset: in-place editing, read-only reasons, truncated→inspector, 2-row diff). Space also CLOSES it (Quick Look grammar). Rationale: two near-identical modals needed every guard fix twice (3 documented incidents). Inspector narrow mode (< 280px store width) hides shortcut hints via a store-derived class, NOT a container query; `container-type` would become the containing block for the fixed `.insp-copy-backdrop` and shrink click-away to the panel.
- 2026-07-22 · **docs/DESIGN.md is the third law file** (pixels; WRITING=words, LESSONS=bugs), born from the founding audit: ~80 hand-authored control rule-sets / 9 species, pressed state on 2, 163 off-grid literals, 8 icon sizes, 5 motion dialects. Enforcement is structural: `<Kbd>` makes wrong glyph forms untypeable (order ⌃⌥⇧⌘, ↩ not ↵, UI font not mono); base classes (.btnish variants, .iconbtn, .linkish) make drifted controls unwritable; `scripts/design-lint.ts` gates the rest (census 597 at birth; flips to --enforce when migration lands). Em dash banned from UI strings (WRITING.md rule 7 amended: split/comma/·; docs keep it). --dur-quick corrected 150→120ms to match the de-facto standard it was competing with.
- 2026-07-22 · **Cancel gets its own transport**: every SSH tunnel pre-spawns a second ssh child (control lane, `ControlMaster=no`): Postgres cancel NEEDS a new connection, and over one multiplexed ssh TCP that connection's handshake queues behind the very row-flood being cancelled (head-of-line blocking). Control paths (token cancel via cancel_query_raw, OOB, terminate) try the lane on sub-deadlines and fall back to the data lane; sessions can hard-abort their own connection (single-fire notifier) so disconnect/terminate/failed-cap-cancel never drain a dead result to completion. Cancel feedback is set synchronously in the UI and cleared only when the run settles; the wire's truth arrives late, the button's truth must not.
- 2026-07-23 · **Match Connection theming**: a Theme Picker card (radio-exclusive with palettes: choosing either clears the other, incl. saving a custom theme) derives the whole theme from the ACTIVE connection's color over a pure-neutral ramp (tint 0); the seed is hex-validated (the sanitize moat) and its hue is STEERED out of the danger band (±15° around red → nearest edge 335/25); Run must never wear Delete's color. connAccent is runtime-only, pushed one-way from the connections store; disconnected falls back to the chosen palette. Colorless profiles hash their id into the avatar rotation (stable for the profile's lifetime); index-derived slots let deleting an unrelated profile retint the app.
- 2026-07-23 · **Stable chrome, refined twice**: strips get fixed heights and reveals occupy layout (visibility, not display); WITHIN-mode swaps must never resize, BETWEEN-mode transitions may reflow; and when a control should hold constant across modes, EQUALIZE its faces by copy first (Cancelling/Connecting dropped the progress …; width-over-register, scoped) then reserve via a stacked grid slot with centered labels. The qb action slot walked stretch→right-align→conditional before landing here; the detour is the documentation.
- 2026-07-24 · **Per-connection themes**: sparse `connThemes: {profileId → {paletteId, match}}`; an absent entry FOLLOWS the app theme, so turning the mode on retints nothing until a pick lands. Picks route by scope (active connection when "One Theme Everywhere" is off, app theme otherwise) and the picker's radio derives from the same resolution, so display scope ≡ write scope by construction. Toggle ON promotes the on-screen choice to the app theme and leaves the map dormant (toggle OFF restores every assignment). Fixed label + switch, never state-swapped text (HIG: the label names the setting, the switch position IS the state; and stable-chrome). Owner dots (absolute overlay) are the pick-landed-here feedback. Stale palette ids degrade to the default palette at rehydrate; same outcome as deleting the theme in-app.
- 2026-07-24 · **Switch species**: `<Switch>`/`.switch` (tokens.css), a macOS-style toggle for feature/setting rows; a hidden native checkbox is the truth (role=switch, Space, label association) and every visual state derives from it via `:has()`; no class mirroring to desync. Spring knob on --ease-spring with the macOS press-stretch (width grows held, springs on release). Checkboxes remain for selection within content (browse filter enable, CM search panels) per HIG.
- 2026-07-24 · **Auto-update over GitHub Releases**: tauri-plugin-updater + minisign (private key and its password live outside the repo, on the release machine and in GitHub Actions secrets; only the pubkey is baked into tauri.conf.json); endpoint = releases/latest/download/latest.json (requires PUBLIC repo); release ritual = bump conf version → tag vX.Y.Z → push (workflow refuses a tag/conf mismatch; a forgotten bump publishes a feed advertising the old version, silently dead). A relaunch is a process death: the updater runs the Quit ceremony (prepareExit in stores/exitGuard.ts, shared verbatim with ⌘Q) before the download and again before the relaunch; staged edits/draft rows/open transactions are never silently lost. Stall watchdog (90s eventless → error) + generation counter so an orphaned download can never surprise-relaunch; installed-but-not-relaunched is its own honest state.
- 2026-08-20 · **Rebuilt-session consent is inline, tiered, single-surface**: the commit preview never stacks a second dialog. A rebuilt session shows an accented chip on the preview itself ("Connection was rebuilt · commits against current state", hover for the full story); it escalates to warn tier + a "Commit Anyway" button ONLY when the dead session held an open transaction (tombstoned by session id in `deadTxSessions` — ids are never reused, so FIFO cap instead of clearing logic). Enter still commits in both tiers: the preview IS the review surface and the row-verified pipeline is the actual safety; the old confirmDanger-on-top mapped Enter to Cancel and ate the commit mid-keystroke. Commit-time rebuilds (between ⌘S and Enter) restamp silently for the same reason.
- 2026-08-21 · **One inner scroller per value, and the pencil is a control**: a structured value in the record view gets exactly ONE scroll surface between the page and the content — the JSON box itself (`.rv-val:has(> .jsonfield)` un-caps the shell; a capped shell around a capped box made every long JSON a two-flick read). The edit pencil is a clickable `.iconbtn`, not a decoration: inline after plain values (negative vertical margins keep the 22px box from growing the row), `.bordered` chip anchored inside the box's bottom-right corner for JSON (on the non-scrolling shell, so it holds still). Editable cells carry NO native title — the header hint + pencil are the affordance; a tooltip trailing the cursor across a scrolling box is noise. Read-only reasons keep their titles.
- 2026-08-21 · **Refresh feedback is honest choreography, not decoration**: ⇧⌘R animates the sidebar db glyph as "the rebuild" — the disc stack splits apart when the heal starts, holds apart while it runs, claps home on the verdict, and the top→bottom accent shine plays ONLY on success (failure settles shine-less; the dot tells the red story). Background heals skip the split and shine only when a dead session was actually found and torn down for rebuild (`healProfile` returns `{ok, rebuilt}`; tab replacements ride the fresh spare lazily, the primary being green is the claim); a no-op probe stays invisible, so the shine stays trustworthy. Timing lives in one place (stores/refreshFx.ts: min-apart so fast verdicts still read, shine lags the clap, generation guard); springs via presets only. AUTO_HEAL_SHINE is a trial flag.

### Agent A1 · W0 (research, 2026-09-05; reports in ~/projects/qwry-agent-lab/docs/research/w0-*.md)
- 2026-09-05 · **AST gate = `pg_query` 6.2.0** (libpg_query 17 grammar), not `sqlparser`: parses the grammar the server runs (no false rejects) and exposes `into_clause` / `locking_clause` / CTE bodies. Measured cost: cold 53s release / 23s debug (bindgen + libclang, auto-found from the Command Line Tools and on GitHub's macos-latest = macOS 26 image), 0.1s no-op check, 0.54ms per 2KB parse. CI caches `target/` on `Cargo.lock`.
- 2026-09-05 · **Gate policy**: allow SELECT / WITH…SELECT / EXPLAIN [ANALYZE] of an allowed SELECT / VALUES / TABLE; refuse data-modifying CTEs, SELECT INTO / CTAS, FOR UPDATE/SHARE (refuse, never strip: the trace must show exactly what ran, §8.4), COPY, SHOW/SET (spec-literal; widening SHOW is an open question), multi-statement input (run_sql = exactly one statement) and unparseable input (parse error text feeds the repair loop). A function deny-list is walked over every FuncCall (pg_terminate/cancel_backend, pg_sleep*, set_config, lo_*, pg_read_file, pg_ls_*, dblink*): measured that `default_transaction_read_only` does NOT stop these. `locking_clause` / `into_clause` are NOT visited by pg_query's `.nodes()` walker: check them on every SelectStmt reached via CTE, set-op branch, FROM subquery, JOIN AND SubLink (`SELECT (SELECT … FOR UPDATE)` slipped the first draft, measured). Measured: read-only also blocks EXPLAIN ANALYZE of a write before any row is touched; bare EXPLAIN of a write returns a plan only, so it is refused as out of scope, not as a write.
- 2026-09-05 · **`claude -p` transport = streamable-HTTP MCP server inside the Rust process** (`rmcp` 3.2.0 `server` + `transport-streamable-http-server` on bare hyper/hyper-util through `TowerToHyperService`, no axum; spike compiled and served a live initialize), one server per app instance on 127.0.0.1:0, one bearer token per agent thread mapped to that thread's dedicated session; not a stdio sidecar (§11 q1 closed). The bearer header is delivered on every MCP request (measured). Never depend on `Mcp-Session-Id` (client 2.1.260 opens with the session-less 2026-07-28 revision and falls back to 2025-11-25); validate Origin; the token dies with the thread.
- 2026-09-05 · **`claude -p` flags are load-bearing**: `--tools ""` (else 26 built-ins incl. SendMessage/CronCreate are reachable) + `--allowedTools 'mcp__qwry__*'` (else every MCP call is permission-denied and the model gives up silently) + `--setting-sources ""` (else the user's own hooks/CLAUDE.md inject context; `--bare` is unusable: it needs an API key) + `--strict-mcp-config --mcp-config` + `--output-format stream-json --verbose [--include-partial-messages]` + `--system-prompt` (drop `--exclude-dynamic-system-prompt-sections`: documented no-op with it) + `--session-id <thread id>` on the first call and `--resume` after; prompt on stdin; direct exec via tokio::process, never a shell. `system/init.mcp_servers[].status != "connected"` is a hard failure before the first model turn (a dead MCP server yields exit 0 and a toolless model). `--max-turns` is per invocation: the TS loop owns the 12-turn thread cap.
- 2026-09-05 · **Provider HTTP goes through Rust** (`agent_http_stream`: the Keychain key is injected server-side, SSE bytes relayed over a Channel, abort by request id): satisfies §8.3 literally (keys never enter TS) and Tauri's http scope cannot allowlist arbitrary user-supplied local hosts/ports. Only Rust talks to the network, as only Rust talks to PG. Connect timeout 10s, idle-read 60s, no total; unreachable → error event carrying a "start the server" hint for local presets.
- 2026-09-05 · **Gemini ships as a preset of the OpenAI-compatible adapter** (`generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/openai/`, bearer), not the native adapter §7 listed third: streaming + tools are documented; the layer is beta and parallel tools undocumented, so promote to native only when a measured failure demands it.
- 2026-09-05 · **Preset quirks are data** (`presets.ts`): Mistral tool_call ids must match `^[A-Za-z0-9]{9}$` (also when routed through OpenRouter/Together: family detected from a model id containing `mistral`); xAI rejects `additionalProperties:false` and needs /chat/completions, not /responses; DeepSeek defaults to `deepseek-v4-flash` (its reasoning model is unreliable with tools); llama-server needs `--jinja`, vLLM `--enable-auto-tool-choice --tool-call-parser`; Ollama ≥ 0.8.0 streams tool calls; local `/v1/models` ids are file paths, derive labels. Measured: LFM2.5-2.6B on llama-server completes one forced tool call with canonical OpenAI chunking; this does not move §3 (multi-turn loops are the small-tier failure).
- 2026-09-05 · **Provider events gain `thinking` and `toolResult`**: OpenAI-compatible runtimes stream `reasoning_content` deltas (measured, ~40 chunks before the tool call) and Anthropic streams `thinking_delta`; both render in the thinking strip / trace, never as answer text. `toolResult` + `Provider.ownsLoop` cover claude -p, which executes tools itself: the loop records and never re-executes.
- 2026-09-05 · **Registry facts**: Sonnet 5, Opus 5 and Opus 4.6/4.7/4.8 reject temperature/top_p/top_k outright (400); Haiku 4.5 accepts; `samplingParams` is per model. Anthropic's minimum cacheable prefix is per model and non-monotonic (Opus 5 512, Sonnet 5 / Opus 4.8 1024, Opus 4.7 2048, Haiku 4.5 / Opus 4.6 4096): `cacheMinTokens` per model, and a miss is silent (check `cache_creation_input_tokens`). Hosted small-parameter models never earn `small` (tier = loop reliability, not RAM). Hand-typed model ids are `verified: false` until a live `/models` call confirms them (`claude-haiku-4.5` vs the real `claude-haiku-4-5` is the failure class); the picker prefers live model lists.
- 2026-09-05 · Claude Code keeps its own transcript under `~/.claude` for `--resume` threads: accepted as a second local copy (ideology 7 holds, nothing leaves the machine); `--no-session-persistence` would kill follow-ups.

### Agent A1 · W1 (build, 2026-09-05)
- 2026-09-05 · **A non-2xx provider response is DATA, not a Rust error**: `agent_http_stream` sends `Start` (status + headers) before any body, follows with an `Error` chunk carrying the provider's own text (capped at `ERROR_BODY_CAP`), and still resolves `Ok(HttpDone)`. `Platform.httpStream` is the single place that turns that into `HttpStatusError` / `HttpUnreachableError`, which it cannot do if the command rejects first — and rejecting first would throw away the provider's message, which is the only useful half of a 400. The adapter maps status plus body to a `ProviderErrorKind`; a 401 never quotes the body (it routinely echoes a key fragment, §8.3).
- 2026-09-05 · **Provider cancellation is a `CancellationToken` keyed on a CALLER-minted `request_id`** (module-level map in `agent_http.rs`, not `AppState`, so an abort works from any handler): TypeScript mints the id before the call, so `agent_http_abort` can land before the first chunk does. A server-minted id leaves that window open. An unknown id is a no-op, never an error.
- 2026-09-05 · **The MCP server is one listener per app instance and one `StreamableHttpService` + call log per THREAD TOKEN** (`agent_mcp.rs`, rmcp on bare hyper via `TowerToHyperService`, no axum): configured stateless (`legacy_session_mode: false`, `json_response: true`, allowed hosts loopback only) so no `Mcp-Session-Id` is ever issued for anything to route on — the CLI opens session-less and the bearer token IS the identity. The token maps to that thread's dedicated read-only session and dies with the thread, so a child calling back into a torn-down thread gets a clean error instead of another thread's database.
- 2026-09-05 · **The MCP result text mirrors `tools.tauri.ts` / `context.ts` byte for byte**, not merely in spirit: `Meta::build`, `describe_text`, `run_text`, `peek_text` and `probe_text` in `agent_mcp.rs` are line-for-line ports of `buildMeta`, `renderDescribe`, `formatRun`, `formatPeek` and `formatProbes`, and both sides' doc comments name the other. Otherwise `claude -p` reads a different app than the OpenAI adapter does and the EVAL numbers stop comparing. Fixed by the port: NULL renders `∅`, a capped result reads `(N rows, capped at 2000; showing M)`, probe blocks are `-- <sql>`.
- 2026-09-05 · **`describe_tables` prefers the comment it just read over the cached snapshot's**, column by column (`ColumnValues.comment` → `renderDescribe` / `describe_text`), so a `COMMENT ON COLUMN` added since the last introspect reaches the model; the CREATE TABLE header comment stays the snapshot's, because that is the one the candidate block (§4.2) quoted and the LEGACY rule was applied to. Both renderers changed together, as they must.
- 2026-09-05 · **`anthropic-version` belongs to the adapter, not the relay**: `outbound_headers` still drops any caller-supplied `authorization` / `x-api-key` (a TypeScript bug must never smuggle a header past the Keychain path) but leaves `anthropic-version` alone and fills in `ANTHROPIC_VERSION` only when the adapter sent none. The adapter is what knows which wire shape it was written against; the two constants agree today and this is what keeps a disagreement from becoming a duplicated header and a 400.
- 2026-09-05 · **`HttpStatusError` carries `retryAfterMs`**: the platform reads the wait the provider STATED (`Retry-After` delta-seconds or HTTP-date on the start chunk, or `HttpChunk.error.retry_after_ms`) and attaches it to the error, because response headers are not reachable from the adapter layer. `http.ts` prefers it and falls back to scraping the body; absent means the provider said nothing there, never that it said zero. A header-derived value travels as a number on the error and never as message text.
- 2026-09-05 · **Every appdb write the agent makes has a read**: `agent_answers_list(thread_id)` joins `agent_answers` to `agent_turns` in turn order, so a reopened thread rebuilds its assumption chips, sanity line and row count instead of showing a turn whose verdict is unrecoverable. `agent_answer_put` alone was a write nothing could read.
- 2026-09-05 · **Agent sessions are gated at the session, not only at the tool**: `PgSession.agent_gated` is set in `open_session(force_read_only)` before the id is registered, and `commands::execute` / `execute_stream` refuse such a session. Verifier finding (S1): `default_transaction_read_only` does not stop `pg_sleep` / `pg_terminate_backend` / `lo_import`, so a raw-SQL command reachable by session id was a side door around the AST gate.
- 2026-09-05 · **Cancel wins over an error body**: an `agent_http_abort` during a non-2xx drain emits `Cancelled`, never the partial provider text as Auth/Rate/Provider (a user cancel used to be recorded as a provider failure).
- 2026-09-05 · **`StopReason` gains `turnCap`; every truncation is `maxTokens`**: `claude -p` `error_max_turns` is a cut-off conversation (the loop reports the turn-cap verdict with Fix It, never "answered" on the empty text); Anthropic `model_context_window_exceeded`, OpenAI `content_filter` / `length` map to `maxTokens`. Trace and persisted tool records keep the provider's own tool name (`ToolName | string`): a stray built-in shows up by name instead of being laundered into the `run_sql` count.
- 2026-09-05 · **A superseded run never clears the live run's flags**: `runInto` clears `busy`/`phase` only when its controller is still the thread's authoritative one, and a cancel pressed while the first ask is still inside `agentConnect` is honoured the moment the session lands (`cancelRequested` set), so ⌘. works during a bastion connect too.
- 2026-09-05 · **NUMERIC compares as decimal text**: `eval/compare.ts` rounds NUMERIC half-up at four places on the digit string (BigInt), never through `Number()`, which collapsed two distinct 18-digit values into one string (a false PASS). FLOAT4/FLOAT8 keep the float rule; the cross-type agreement tests still hold.
- 2026-09-05 · **Unmeasured is not green** (EVAL.md §4): the CI gate fails when no baseline row exists for the run's bench + provider + model + prompt version; every recorded row is `claude-code`, so configuring a hosted `EVAL_API_KEY` begins with one deliberate local run and a baseline commit.
- 2026-09-05 · **W1 fixers were applied by the maintainer session, not by the workflow**: five parallel Opus fixers each made two tool calls and then hung until the harness's 3-minute stall detector killed them (six attempts, ~2.7 h); the findings carried exact fixes, so they were applied directly. Long-running commands in workflow prompts run in the background and are polled.

### Agent A1 · W2 (design lock, 2026-09-05; plan in ~/projects/qwry-agent-lab/docs/research/w2-ui-plan.md)
- 2026-09-05 · **The Ask card's UI state lives in `src/stores/ask.ts`**, persisted like `useInspector` (open + width only), not in `src/stores/agent.ts`: the agent store is loop-facing, written around LESSONS 3, and unpersisted; a persisted width inside it would force a `partialize` around the whole thread map. Thread identity (`activeThread` per profile) stays in agent.ts, where W1 put it. The card defaults CLOSED, unlike the inspector: a panel appearing uninvited after an update is the wrong first impression.
- 2026-09-05 · **Width budget = 480px for the main card, both directions**, enforced as a transition effect in App.tsx on `askOpen`/`inspectorOpen` (measured on the first frame, before the opening card has taken its width) so every open path (⌘J, ⌘I, menu, palette, titlebar) obeys without each caller knowing the rule.
- 2026-09-05 · **One grid species (AGENT-UX §2.4, item 3 of §2 at the time): `Grid` grows a `readOnly` prop, never a second grid.** In readOnly the grid renders the `statement` it is handed and touches none of `useEdits`/`useBrowser`/`useResults`/`useGridFilter`/`useFind`/`useGridStats`/`useInspector`: those are singletons indexed by the ACTIVE RESULTS TAB, so a standalone grid publishing to or reading from them paints one dataset's coordinates over another (LESSONS 4). Inspector targeting, ⌘F find, the status-bar quick-filter and selection stats are therefore OFF for Ask grids in W2; `colTypes` and `filterText` props are the scoped replacements. Editing, insert, delete, undo, paste, FK navigation are off; selection, copy/export, sort, hide, record view, keyboard nav stay.
- 2026-09-05 · **Esc inside the card is a ladder, never a close**: trace → picker → blur the composer. ⌘J mirrors ⌘I's focus discipline (capture on open, restore on close, focus pulled out of a collapsed card from any path). Focus is state-owned (LESSONS 7): `useAsk.focusSeq`, consumed by the lazy AskPanel with rAF so escStack's focus-restore lands first and loses. ⌘. cancels the Ask turn only when focus is inside `.ask-fixed` and a turn is busy; the menu accelerator path in App.tsx applies the same test, loading the agent store lazily so the editor's cancel stays instant.
- 2026-09-05 · **The READ-ONLY badge is the titlebar chip's LOCKED (ok) tint, always**; the sketch's danger toggle for prod is not reproduced: danger on that chip means writes on, which Ask can never be (AGENT-SPEC §8.1). Wording follows the chip: `READ-ONLY`, `PROD · READ-ONLY`.
- 2026-09-05 · **Species assignments**: assumption chips = Chip/pill toggle (`aria-pressed`); starter and follow-up chips = the dashed-ghost species in pill shape (a question not yet asked; hover turns the dash solid accent), rendered by ONE component (`FollowUps`) in both places so the morph motion is shared; thinking-strip chips are status pills until their call finishes, then buttons that open the trace; the send/stop control is the icon-button species at the 28px tier with the one accent fill (the sketch's 26px is off both the grid and the tier).
- 2026-09-05 · **Follow-ups render only when the loop supplies them**: `FollowUps` receives `questions={[]}` until `AskAnswer` carries `followUps` (requested of the agent owner); the UI never invents questions client-side. Starters in the empty state come from `starters.ts` over the schema's real nouns (LEGACY tables and partitions excluded, the generic row count never).
- 2026-09-05 · **Fix It and Retry are store actions in agent.ts** (`fixIt(exchangeId, sql)`, `retry(exchangeId)`), the same re-run shape as `toggleAssumption`: capture before await, `rearm`, `runInto`. Fix It hands the model the question, the failure text and the user's edited SQL as a starting point; it never rewrites someone else's query silently. Retry persists the user turn only when the failed run never reached `persist()`.
- 2026-09-05 · **Per-block provenance rides the footer** (AGENT-UX §9): the sketch shows the connection once in the header; the footer's first fragment carries the 14px avatar and name so every block speaks for its data even when scrolled away from the header.
- 2026-09-05 · **Thinking-strip chips group by label** (`probe ×2`, `run ×3`): consecutive calls with the same verb and identifier collapse into one chip that runs while any member runs and errs when any member erred; clicking it opens the trace at the FIRST call's step (the group's id is that call's id, which is also the trace step id). Chips are status pills while the exchange streams and become trace buttons once it lands.
- 2026-09-05 · **Three `readOnly` gates in Grid beyond the W2 plan**: the infinite-scroll `nearEndHook` never fires from a standalone grid (it would page the ACTIVE results tab), a TSV copy above `COPY_ASYNC_CELLS` runs synchronously instead of through the results-pane copy job, and an export failure reports through the copy cue toast (`export failed`) because there is no results pane to carry `globalError`; LESSONS 4 and 9. `RecordView` gained the same `readOnly` prop for the same reason: its staged-edit lookup is keyed by statement index, which a standalone grid's `index: 0` collides with, so a readOnly record view reads no pending edits and offers no edit hint.
- 2026-09-05 · **One error event per verdict, kind decided by the verdict**: `finish()` in loop.ts is the only emitter; a `failed` verdict WITH SQL is `sql` (editable SQL + Fix It), one WITHOUT is `provider` (Retry), and the stated wait (`retryAfterMs`) rides the event to `Exchange.error`. The loop used to emit `sql` and then `provider` for the same failure, and the store keeps the last one, so every SQL failure reached the UI as a provider error. The loop test asserts exactly one error event and its kind.
- 2026-09-05 · **Follow-ups are a separate post-verdict call, not a loop turn** (`src/agent/followups.ts`, `AskAnswer.followUps`): the store fires it after an `answered` verdict lands, on the run's own signal, and patches the exchange only while that same answer object is still on screen, so the answer never waits for the suggestions and the eval loop is unchanged. It is recorded as a `followups` trace step (prompt sent, raw reply, questions parsed) because nothing sent to a provider is hidden (AGENT-SPEC 8.4); it never runs for `ownsLoop` providers (a `claude -p` side call would `--resume` the thread and write itself into the conversation); v1 uses the thread's own model, the registry having no cost axis for "cheapest". Not persisted (appdb has no column): a reopened thread recomputes them for its newest answered exchange only.
- 2026-09-05 · **Base URL overrides are settings** (`useSettings.agentBaseUrls`, sparse, the preset default never stored): `modelChoice()` carries `baseUrl` so the loop's provider reaches the URL Settings › Models probed; `baseUrlFor()` reads the same map for the probes and group headings; the Settings field commits on blur or ↩ and the setup card commits the URL that answered.
- 2026-09-05 · **A reloaded failed exchange shows its failure block**: `openThread` maps `agent_answers.status` (`failed` → `sql` when SQL was recorded, else `provider`; `turn_cap` → `turncap`; `cancelled`) and prefers the recorded SQL over re-extracting it from the text. appdb keeps neither the error text nor the turn count, so the messages say only what is known (`the query failed. The error text is not kept in the history`, `stopped at the turn cap`), LESSONS 9 over invented detail.
- 2026-09-05 · **Re-runs start from a clean text** and **a busy thread refuses a new run**: `rearm()` clears `text`/`thinking` (deltas append, so the previous prose was becoming the new answer's prefix) and keeps the previous `answer` until the new one replaces it; `ask()`, `toggleAssumption()` and `locateExchange()` return early while `busy[threadId]`, so no caller can abort the live run through the controller swap. A provider failure on a re-run leaves the previous answer's SQL in the exchange; AnswerBlock hands FailureBlock `sql: null` for that kind so the old statement is never offered as the one that failed. A cancelled run keeps its SqlRow (the failure block shows only `cancelled` for it, AGENT-UX 7).
- 2026-09-05 · **`spring.layout` is the shared-layout preset** AGENT-UX 10 names (PANEL-class: the suggestion → echo morph travels and settles rather than snapping); **14 joins the avatar exceptions** in DESIGN rule 5 as the inline provenance mark (Ask header and footer); **`.badge-ok`** in tokens.css is the LOCKED tint (`prod-chip.locked`, 12%), worn by the READ-ONLY badge and the `large` tier (the `mid` tier wears `.badge-accent`), replacing two local color-mix tints.
- 2026-09-05 · **`Manage in Settings › Models…` lands on the section**: `setSettingsOpen(open, section?)` carries a runtime-only `settingsSection`; ModelsSettings scrolls its heading into view on mount with the default (instant) `scrollIntoView`, never animated; the section clears when the modal closes.
- 2026-09-05 · **Finished chips open in the drawer before the answer lands**: `toolEnd` carries `args` and `result`, `ToolChip` keeps them, and the drawer renders a finished chip through the same tool-row renderer as a trace step; a running chip stays a static row. Context, turn and verdict rows still arrive with the answer.
- 2026-09-05 · **Path-shaped model ids match the registry on their file name** (`baseModelId` in registry.ts, used by `modelInfo`/`tierOf`): llama-server reports `/…/LFM2.5-2.6B-Q4_K_M.gguf`, and without this the loop gated it as `mid` and ran the tool loop on a small model; `listModels` therefore labels and tiers such a path from its registry row.
- 2026-09-05 · **The width budget binds the drag as much as the open**: both side-card resize handlers cap the dragged width at `main + current - 480` from a live `.main-card` measurement (`sideCardDragCap`, App.tsx), never under the card's current width, so a drag can shrink a card in an already-tight window but never widen one into the main card. The transition effect alone left the main card squeezable to nothing once both cards were open (review finding, AGENT-UX §1).
- 2026-09-05 · **The composer swallows only unmodified typing keys** (LESSONS 10): Esc and every ⌘/⌃/⌥ chord bubble; ↩ sends, ⇧↩ newlines, `isComposing` respected. A click inside the card focuses the panel root unless the target is an input, textarea, select, contenteditable or the grid (which focuses itself), so WKWebView's no-focus-on-click buttons still leave the card as the chord target. Composer drafts are per connection (`useAsk.drafts`, keyed by the on-screen `draftFor`): a draft typed toward one connection never surfaces in another's composer and comes back when its connection does (LESSONS 4).
- 2026-09-05 · **AskPanel pushes `setActiveProfile` + `loadThreads` on connection change** (provenance structural, LESSONS 4); the thread list is a ContextMenu (menu-row species) with `Delete Current Thread…` behind `confirmDanger`; a trace target whose exchange left the active thread closes itself.
- 2026-09-05 · **Scroll and reveal**: a new exchange pins the thread scroller to the bottom instantly in a layout effect; streaming growth never auto-scrolls; nothing animates scroll or typing. Answer sections fade in with `--dur-slow` opacity via `.ans > *`, never height. The collapsed card and the closed trace drawer get `visibility: hidden` after `--dur-slow` (a `visibility 0s linear var(--dur-slow)` delay that survives the reduced-motion kill) so hidden controls leave the tab order (ROADMAP gotcha) while the width transition still runs.
- 2026-09-05 · **The Ask grid slot** is `GRID_HEADER_H + min(rows, 6) × gridRowHeight(density) + 2` (its own two hairlines, border-box), `<Grid key={exchange.id} statement readOnly />` with the statement memoised on `(run, sql)`; a zero-row run renders the status line (`0 rows · N ms`) and no grid, since header-only chrome over nothing is dead space (DESIGN rule 2). Numeric alignment without column types sniffs the first 50 non-null values per column against the stats effect's own shape (`NUM_SHAPE`, a module constant mirroring that regex rather than hoisting it); a host that hands `colTypes` gets both the tokio short forms and the `format_type` long forms treated as numeric.
- 2026-09-05 · **The polite live region is the LATEST block, not only the streaming one** (`aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="false"`), so the final `answer.text` replacement is still announced; older blocks are `off`. Streamed text is plain pre-wrap text, no per-character work.
- 2026-09-05 · **Thinking strip overflow**: fixed 24px, never wraps; every chip arrival and strip resize assigns `scrollLeft = scrollWidth` (an instant assignment, never animated) so the newest chip stays visible, and an `.overflow` class masks the left edge so clipped older chips fade instead of cutting a pill. The `·` more-marker shows while the exchange streams and no tool is running (the model is thinking). Chips enter through the `menuIn` preset, which is `spring.snappy` (AGENT-UX §10); their element identity never changes when a running pill becomes a trace button, so finishing does not replay the entrance.
- 2026-09-05 · **SqlRow's expanded body is a read-only CodeMirror 6 view** (`sql({dialect: PostgreSQL})`, `qwryHighlight`, `qwryTheme`, `Prec.high` override for auto height / `--text-sm` / `--lh-data`, `lineWrapping`, `readOnly` + `editable(false)`), mounted in a layout effect so the reveal is one frame and remounted on theme flip like the editor. `Copy SQL` fires the copy cue; `Open in Tab` is `useTabs.newTab(sql, title)`, which selects the tab and never auto-runs. Neither carries an ellipsis: both act at once (WRITING rule 2).
- 2026-09-05 · **Follow-up chips morph by `layoutId = ask-q:<question>`** into the LATEST AnswerBlock's echo (a `motion.div` with the same id), so a question typed twice never pairs two echoes; `FollowUps` takes `disabled` and receives `busy` from AnswerBlock. Empty sections (`FollowUps`, `AssumptionChips`, `SanityLine`) return null so `.ans`'s flex gap never reserves a slot for an absent part.
- 2026-09-05 · **The trace drawer is NOT on escStack**: its capture listener makes every window chord stand down, right for a modal, wrong for a non-modal panel inside the card (⌘J/⌘W must keep working with the trace open). A scoped keydown on the drawer root owns exactly two keys, Tab (wraps among the drawer's tabbables, the inspector-style trap AGENT-UX §12 asks for) and Esc (closes, `preventDefault` + `stopPropagation` so AskPanel's ladder does not double-handle); every chord bubbles (LESSONS 10). Focus choreography (LESSONS 7): on open the opener is captured and rAF focuses the asked-for step's header button or the drawer root; on close rAF returns focus to the opener if still connected, else the panel root, standing down when `useAsk.open` is false (App.tsx owns the collapse restore) or when something else took focus deliberately. Drawer mousedown focuses the drawer root unless the target is a button, so Tab starts inside; body text stays selectable.
- 2026-09-05 · **Trace reveal and rows**: opening from a chip or sanity fragment expands `tool:<id>` and `scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'})` (instant); the footer link expands the first step (context). A fresh exchange resets the expanded set; a second chip on the same open exchange adds to it. Rows are context / turn N / tool / verdict in the sketch's kind colours (tier 2 / `--syn-keyword` / `--accent` / `--ok`); turn rows summarise the tool calls that followed (`describe_tables(users, orders) · peek_values(users.is_deleted)`) else the turn text's first line; tool rows are `<code>name</code> summary` (`run_sql 12 rows` parsed from the `(N rows)` line) with ` · error` in danger; the verdict row reads `answered · 12 rows · 3 assumptions · 1 warning`. Bodies: context = the exact block sent; turn = thinking then text; tool = args (run_sql/probe SQL shown as SQL, not escaped JSON) then result; verdict = assumptions + message/SQL. Mono, pre-wrap, 220px cap, `user-select: text`.
- 2026-09-05 · **Trace timing shows what was recorded, nothing derived**: `48 ms` / `2.9 s` per step, tabular and right-aligned; the verdict row reads `17.6 s total` because the loop records elapsed-since-start there. Reloaded threads (the store writes `turns: 0` and tool ms 0) hide the ms cells and the turns fragment rather than print `0 ms` (LESSONS 9). The drawer header mirrors the footer: `5 turns · 17.6s · 2 probes` (probes = tool steps named probe; zero fragments omitted). The footer note reads `Everything shown here is what qwry sent and received. Claude Code adds its own harness prefix that cannot be shown.` only when `exchange.provider === 'claude-code'`, else `Everything the model received is shown here.`
- 2026-09-05 · **FailureBlock's SQL field is CodeMirror** (the JsonField precedent, not the sketch's contenteditable): `sql({dialect: PostgreSQL})`, `qwryHighlight`, history, defaultKeymap, drawSelection, lineWrapping, 220px scroll cap, aria-label `SQL to fix`; the CM modules are chunks the editor already loads. ⌘↩ inside the field is Fix It at `Prec.highest` (App.tsx respects `defaultPrevented`, so the window's Run accelerator does not also fire); Esc hands focus to `.ask-panel`, never body; a new last attempt replaces the doc outside the undo history. Per kind: `sql` = danger-soft `role=alert` box, uppercase `stopped after N turns` (`query failed` at 0), the PG message with double-quoted identifiers rendered as mono `.ask-id` (quotes dropped: data wears data's clothes), `DETAIL:`/`HINT:` on their own lines under a tier-2 uppercase key, the field, then `Fix It ⌘↩` (primary, disabled while busy or blank) / `Open in Tab` / `Ask Differently`; `turncap` = the loop's `stopped after 12 turns` title plus the same field and actions when a SQL was tried; `provider` = `stopped`, the provider sentence verbatim (it already carries the next step), `Retry` primary and a live `try again in 20s` countdown from `retryAfterMs`; `cancelled` = `cancelled` alone in the status register.
- 2026-09-05 · **The model picker popover is an `AnchoredOverlay`** (src/app/overlay/Overlay.tsx), the app's non-modal anchored primitive (portal to body, escStack so Esc and outside click close it, viewport clamp, topmost-only `onKey`), not an in-card absolute panel; it hangs from the pill's right edge (`rect.right - 280`, `rect.bottom + 4`, `transform-origin: top right`). Keyboard nav (↑↓ Home End ↩) runs through `onKey` with a `.hot` row like ContextMenu; the menu is focusless (mousedown `preventDefault`) so escStack restores focus to the panel root on close, and AskPanel's Esc ladder still converges through `onClose`.
- 2026-09-05 · **Tier bubbles have a keyboard route** (DESIGN rule 8): a real styled `role=tooltip` span, a SIBLING of the badge positioned against the nearest positioned ancestor (the pill, `max-width: 220px` so it stays inside a 320px card; the popover row, 240px). It opens on hover, on the pill's focus (suppressed while its popover is open), and on a popover row when the arrow keys make it hot, never when the pointer does; the badge itself is never focusable (tabindex inside a button is invalid, the menu is focusless). Text `<b>tier</b> · <sentence>`; an unknown model reads `<b>mid</b> · unverified model, treated as mid · <sentence>` behind a `mid?` badge face. Sentences are AGENT-SPEC §3's, lowercase status fragments (small: writes the SQL in one shot with a repair loop and never calls tools; mid: runs probes but may not act on what they show; large: runs probes and acts on their evidence).
- 2026-09-05 · **Local-runtime probes in Settings guard on probe currency, not component liveness** (`probed[id] === url` after the await): liveness let StrictMode strand the chip on `checking` and let a slower stale probe overwrite a newer result.
- 2026-09-05 · **Picker groups** (`modelSources.groupsFrom`): Claude Code always (`· subscription`); every hosted provider with a Keychain key (`· key saved`); every local runtime whose live `/models` probe answered (`· 127.0.0.1:8080`); the currently chosen provider even when it did neither (`· no key` / `· not running`, its row kept visible above the hand-off so the pill is explained); and ONE teaching group, Anthropic without a key. A provider whose Keychain/probe state has not answered yet appears only when chosen, titled `· checking`, so nothing paints and then vanishes; every other provider is a Settings matter behind `Manage in Settings › Models…`. Selection writes `setAgentConnModel(profileId, …)` and ALSO `setAgentModel` when no app default exists yet, so `modelChoice()` resolves at once (the exchange records provider/model at ask time; there is no separate current model).
- 2026-09-05 · **`src/ask/modelSources.ts` is the one place three components share provider knowledge** (labels, auth kind, `PROVIDER_ORDER`, `shortLabel` ('Claude Haiku 4.5' → 'Haiku 4.5', strips ' (local)'), `contextHint`, `TIER_SENTENCE`, `defaultModelFor`, `probeLocal` with a 3s race timeout answering `[]` on silence, `loadKeys`, `groupsFrom`, `encode/decodeChoice` for select values) instead of ~80 duplicated lines each.
- 2026-09-05 · **Settings › Models** lists ALL providers in `PROVIDER_ORDER` as a 3-column grid (name · field · status) inside the existing settings-section skin, mounted after the Query section via `React.lazy` + Suspense (fallback = the `Models` heading so the modal does not jump) to keep the provider registry out of the cold-start path. Key fields are `type=password` `autoComplete=new-password`, save on blur/↩, clear after save, status `saved` + `Forget Key` linkish, else `no key` / `checking` / `not saved` (danger); saving a key while no app default exists adopts the provider's default model, so Ask becomes usable from Settings too. URL fields probe on blur/↩ (skipped when unchanged) → `1 model` / `N models` / `not running` / `checking`. Claude Code's row reads `No key needed` / `uses your subscription` (no CLI-presence IPC exists). Defaults: `Model for <conn in mono>` with an `App default` option and `App Default`, options `Haiku 4.5 · mid` grouped per provider from the same `groupsFrom`.
- 2026-09-05 · **SetupCard**: provider select (Claude Code first, `· your subscription`; local presets `· local`; routers without registry rows excluded, since a key alone yields no model to ask with), then `Key` + `Save Key`, or `Base URL` + `Save URL`, or `Use Claude Code`; save writes the Keychain, then `setAgentModel(provider, defaultModelFor)` and `onConfigured` → `useAsk.requestFocus()`. The note line is ONE fixed slot: the Keychain sentence at rest, the error register on failure (`nothing answered at 127.0.0.1:8080. Start it with llama-server --jinja`), so feedback never moves the form (DESIGN rule 2, LESSONS 9). Claude Code's default model is claude-haiku-4-5, the sketch's active row. Registers: menu rows Title Case (`Add a Key in Settings…`, not the sketch's `Add a key in Settings…`), status chips lowercase, the sketch's parenthetical option faces (`Claude Code (your subscription, no key)`) became `·` faces (WRITING rule 3), and the `claude` command name wears mono (`.ask-id`).
- 2026-09-05 · **Starters are de-snaked prose over a deterministic pool**: nouns from table names (`order_v2` → orders, `wardrobe_products_v2` → wardrobe products, a naive pluraliser on the last word); pool = top 8 by reltuples ∪ top 4 FK hubs (inbound FK count), ties broken by name so one snapshot always yields the same three; migration ledgers (schema_migrations, alembic_version, flyway, knex, ar_internal_metadata, spatial_ref_sys) excluded alongside LEGACY twins, partitions and system schemas. Shapes: `How many orders were created each month this year?`, `Which users have the most orders?`, `How are orders split by payment status?`, filling with `What are the 20 most recently created orders?` / `What do 10 sample X look like?`. Locked by starters.test (determinism, no underscore, parent → child, `noun()`).
- 2026-09-05 · **Sanity fragments name their producing call** (`SanityFragment.stepId`, loop.ts): a peek records `{ column, id }` and `sanityLine` emits `checked <col> values` with that id, a probe fragment is pushed as `{ ...fragment, stepId: call.id }`, and `sanity_json` round-trips it. `sanityStep()` returns the id when the trace carries a tool step with it and falls back to the structural text/SQL match only for fragments persisted before the field existed, so the section-4 click is structural (LESSONS 4) rather than text-matched; loop.test asserts every emitted fragment's id is a trace step. Cancelled and provider-failed verdicts now pass `sanity` too: the tool step stays in the trace, so what it found stays in the answer.
- 2026-09-05 · **The `claude -p` child gets an env ALLOWLIST, and `USER` is on it**: PATH, HOME, USER, LOGNAME, TMPDIR, LANG, CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR. Measured with `env -i`: PATH + HOME alone yields "Not logged in · Please run /login" because the CLI keys its Keychain credentials on the username; adding USER signs in. Still never the whole environment (a stray API key in a dev shell must not reach the child); a unit test pins the list. Found in the maintainer's first taste-pass Ask.
- 2026-09-05 · **The MCP gate declines `server/discover` (MCP 2026-07-28) on purpose**: rmcp 3.2.0 and Claude Code 2.1.261 both speak the new revision and together lose the tools: the probe succeeds, the CLI asks `tools/list` four times, gets the five tools each time, and still starts the model with an empty tool list. A toolless Haiku then writes `<function_calls>` as prose and invents the rows (seen in the first taste pass). The gate answers the probe with JSON-RPC -32601, the CLI falls back to `initialize` at 2025-11-25 (the revision W0 and the eval baseline measured), and `claude_p_end_to_end_lists_and_calls_the_tools` (opt-in, `QWRY_TEST_CLAUDE=1`) drives the real CLI at the real server to pin it. `QWRY_MCP_TRACE=1` dumps the wire.
