### Agent · F1 (the drawing's tools, 2026-09-18; evidence: the maintainer's recording, the hover probe at 640/960/1280, three green suites)
- 2026-09-18 · **The finding.** The maintainer recorded the drawing widget for 60s and wrote: "the canvas drawing widget tools are buggy." The recording (`qwry-agent-lab/docs/research/f1-recording/`) shows a hand over the sheet's top-right with no cluster on screen (`c_48`, `c_77`, `c_78`), a press there lifting the widget a row down (`c_49`–`c_51`), a press meant to draw opening `More` instead (`c_37`, `c_46`, `c_81`), and the cluster appearing only after an accidental press on a button nothing had drawn.
- 2026-09-18 · **Root cause 1, reversed as a reversal: C2b's `:focus-within`-only carve-out (§16x) hid live controls.** `drawing.css:69` (`.blk-draw:hover:not(:focus-within):not([data-hot]) > .acts-float { opacity: 0 }`) and its `.cvg-handle` twin at `:111` dropped the cluster and the corner handle to `opacity: 0` on a bare hover with no `pointer-events: none`: seven invisible buttons and a 16px invisible handle went on taking presses. AGENT-UX §16x's carve-out is reversed, not amended: the drawing now takes canvas.css's one register (hover, focus-within, `[data-hot]`) with nothing added locally, and the carve-out's real subject is a state — `.blk.blk-draw[data-inking] > .acts-float` and `.cvg-slot:has(.blk-draw[data-inking]) .cvg-handle` hide and disarm only for the length of a stroke in flight (pointerdown to up/cancel/Esc).
- 2026-09-18 · **Root cause 1b, scoped app-wide (LESSONS 18, new): a hidden control took presses everywhere, not only on the drawing.** `.acts-float` (`ask.css`) and `.cvg-handle` (`grid.css`) now carry `pointer-events: none` at rest, `auto` on all five `.acts-float` reveal rules and both `.cvg-handle` reveal rules; the keyboard route is untouched since focus is not a hit test and `:focus-within` re-arms the cluster the instant Tab lands.
- 2026-09-18 · **Root cause 2: a press never focused the sheet.** `Drawing.tsx`'s `onDown` called `e.preventDefault()` on pointerdown before anything focused the sheet (`tabIndex -1`), which cancels the browser's own default (the focus change), so `:focus-within` and the tool chords (`p r o l a t`, `⌘Z`, `⇧⌘Z`) were unreachable by pointer. Fixed: `armSheet(node, root.current)` calls `sheet.current.focus({ preventScroll: true })` immediately in `onDown` (LESSONS 7, the canvas is a scroller), and `restSheet` in the gesture's own `done()` — the one path up, cancel, lost capture and Esc all take. The `full` branch arms the sheet too with no `data-inking`, since the next thing a hand does on paper that is full is `⌘Z`.
- 2026-09-18 · **Root cause 3: the tap — verified as already correct, no code change.** `strokes.ts`'s `penHead` has emitted `M x y l0 0` since C2b, so a one-point pen stroke already renders a zero-length segment under a round cap (a visible dot), and `simplify([x, y])` returns both numbers unchanged; a tap with a shape tool commits nothing at all (`seal`'s `!g.moved` guard). `inkSaid` was never counting an invisible stroke.
- 2026-09-18 · **Evidence.** `src/canvas/__tests__/f1drawing.test.ts` (the CSS law read off the stylesheets, the arm/rest seam, the dot); `src/harness/fixtures.f1.ts`'s `f1-draw-inking` state (PRESSED, not drawn: the product's own pointerdown + two pointermoves, gesture left open), 30 frames at 640/960/1280 in both themes in `qwry-agent-lab/docs/research/f1-frames/`, `c2-draw-tools` unchanged; `docs/research/f1-frames/f1-probe.ts` over CDP, 54/54 PASS across 640/960/1280 (`f1-probe.log`) including `⌘Z` taking back a stroke the POINTER made (10 → 9, impossible before this fix) and `r` after a pointer press arming the rectangle. ### Agent · F2 (the chart dialog, 2026-09-18; evidence: the sketch and its stills, the frames at 640/960/1280, three green suites)

- 2026-09-18 · **The maintainer's own words, taken as the ask:** "for adding graph we can give some sort of guided GUI modal for adding one currently it just adds prompt to ask which user could already do that wont work." A chart used to mean typing a request into Ask; this wave gives it a picker.
- 2026-09-18 · **The composer, `src/canvas/chartSql.ts`, pure and store-free.** `quoteIdent` double-quotes an identifier only when it fails `/^[a-z_][a-z0-9_$]*$/` or is a reserved word (`count` is not, so `as count` stands bare, matching the sketch); inner quotes double. `columnKindOf` is the one type classifier (numeric/date/text; strips a type's `(10,2)`; an array reads as text since nothing in one sums). `chartSql` builds a text group as `select <g>, count(*) as count from <s>.<t> group by 1 order by 2 desc limit N` (a measure column swaps in `sum(<c>) as sum_<c>` etc.) and a date group as `to_char(date_trunc('<unit>', <c>), '<fmt>')` wrapped `select * from (… order by 1 desc limit N) t order by 1` so the newest N rows land oldest-to-newest for the line face; nulls ride through untouched. The same module gives the landed widget's title (`chartTitle`: `` count by `status` ``, `` sum `amount` by `status` ``, `` avg `amount` by month of `created_at` ``) and `chartAskWords`, the `Ask instead` sentence. 25 tests, `src/canvas/__tests__/chartSql.test.ts`.
- 2026-09-18 · **The store door, `src/stores/canvas.ts` `addChart(canvasId, { title, sql, run })`.** One new door, not a special case of an old one: a `result` block, face `chart`, question `""`, prose `""`, chips `[]`, columns/rows/status through the same `capRun` every other write already takes, no `wroteBy` (the dialog is the person's own act, LESSONS 4), placed by the existing `place()` at `defaultSpanFor` exactly as `addNote`/`addDrawing` place theirs, landing on `panelIn`. `askForChart` gained an optional `words` argument so `Ask instead` can hand it the picks in the model's own sentence shape. `newChart()` is the palette's door onto the dialog: it creates a canvas first exactly as `newDrawing` does, reads the new id before the dynamic import that reaches the dialog store (LESSONS 3), and never lets `canvas.ts` import the dialog back.
- 2026-09-18 · **The dialog's own store, `src/stores/chartDialog.ts`.** Open flag, picks, session and run in one store (the `editing` precedent, since the palette reaches the dialog from outside the canvas component that owns it). One `agentConnect` on open; `agentRunReadonly(session, sql, CHART_ROW_CAP + 1, statementTimeout)` on every pick, debounced 250ms behind a run token so a stale answer is dropped; disconnect on close. `schedule()` writes `running: true` synchronously in the pick's own frame and only then starts the debounce timer (LESSONS 16, E3's rule: the running state shows before anything is awaited); `fire()` snapshots the session and the timeout before its first await. `addStands` is the one authority both `↩` and the Add Chart button read.
- 2026-09-18 · **The surface, `src/canvas/ChartDialog.tsx`, and one new species.** `ChartDialogBody` takes the dialog's state as a prop, wired to the store one component out, because zustand v5 hands `renderToStaticMarkup` the store's INITIAL state — a store-connected card would draw an empty dialog in a harness shot whatever it has picked. `Field` (`.field`/`.field-pop`) is the new control DESIGN rule 1 asks for: a bordered field wearing the picked value in mono with the row's own ctx faint beside it, one chevron, full state matrix (rest/hover/active while its popover stands/focus-visible/disabled via `--o-disabled`). Its popover list is NOT re-authored — it reaches down into ask.css's own `.picker-pop`/`.picker-item`/`.picker-group` so one register still owns every mention/field list in the app. The Table field opens the moment the dialog mounts, caret already in its search. Per (Day/Week/Month/Year, Month default) stands only for a date group, and the row below it relabels Top as Last. The preview is `Chart.tsx` itself, unmodified, on `previewBlock()` with no span (bars lie at the pane's 24px pitch, a line takes the whole 132px `PREVIEW_LINE_H`), inside one hairline capped and scrolling past 156/160px so the modal itself never scrolls at the 800×600 floor; the box is absent until a chart exists, and lands on `panelIn` with the rows below it moving on `spring.layout`.
- 2026-09-18 · **The two doors.** `CanvasTab.tsx`'s `+` menu keeps its `Chart…` row (ellipsis unmoved, WRITING rule 2: it still asks before it acts) but the row now calls `openChartDialog(canvasId)` instead of prefilling the composer. `Palette.tsx` gains `New Chart…` beside `New Drawing`, through the port's new `newChart()`. `askForChart` stays exactly as it was, reachable now only from the dialog's own `Ask instead` link.
- 2026-09-18 · **The harness, `src/harness/fixtures.f2.ts`, six states, all pressed rather than drawn:** `f2-chart-open` (the table popover standing, `ord` typed through the real value setter, eight tables seeded across `public`/`analytics`), `f2-chart-preview` (`order_v2` by `status`, count, bars, `8 rows · 241.6 ms`), `f2-chart-date` (`created_at` per Month, avg `amount`, Last 12, a line), `f2-chart-running` (the widget's own cycling skeleton, not a spinner), `f2-chart-error` (a `statuss` column that does not exist), `f2-chart-added` (the widget landed on the canvas, dialog gone). `tauriShim.ts`'s `f2Answer` answers `agent_run_readonly` by the composed statement and falls through to `COMPARE_ROWS` for everything else already covered.

A regression test locks the override in (`chartDialog.test.tsx`, "the box clears the face it holds"); all 36 frames re-shot after the fix rather than left standing on the pre-fix pixels.

### Agent · F3 (the tool island and the select tool, 2026-09-18; evidence: the playable sketch and its stills, the frames, the motion and select probes at 640/960/1280, three green suites)

- 2026-09-18 · **The maintainer's own words, taken as the ask:** "i want it to look more like excalidraw and feel too many (making it feel might be a bit too much)"; "the tools thing can go on top left other thing stay on right"; "we should add pointer/move tool to click drag resize rotate"; the proximity-and-expand choreography described at length and asked to "look absolutely beautiful with transitions and animations"; "each of these can have small shortcut letters on bottom left to teach users their shortcuts"; "it must pass my taste gate so i hope you do EXCEPTIONALLY GOOD LOOKING/ANIMTED/TRANSITION work"; "the vision is not really a strict end goal but a guidance, need practicality too, but dont make it simple." Fable took the island and integration seats on this wave at the maintainer's own explicit ask for exceptional motion, with a playable sketch (`qwry-agent-lab/docs/draw-tools-sketch-f3.html`) as the spec and reference implementation rather than prose alone.
- 2026-09-18 · **The choreography is PORTED, not paraphrased, and then corrected where one clock could not hold what four independent CSS transitions used to.** The sketch's folded pose (`--rest-y: 4px`, scale .9, opacity 0) is kept as every tool's rest slot, so siblings fan out from under the armed tool on open and fly back under it on fold while the armed tool alone travels; but each tool now owns its own position/scale/opacity as motion values (`Fold`, a layout effect) rather than one shared lead, and every tool's position is clamped against the body's own growing height (`min(max(own, PAD), body - LEAD_INSET)`, floor first) so a spring's own overshoot can never poke a tool past the body's inner padding: the sketch's four independent CSS clocks could drift out of sync in exactly that way and this tree cannot. Same clamp on an arm's members against the arm body's width. `springs.ts` gains exactly two getters for this: `spring.rail` (the existing RAIL constant, exposed, not a new number) and `spring.quick` (0.12s, the stylesheet's own `--dur-quick`/`--ease-std` restated in seconds, DESIGN rule 6); the 16ms-per-slot stagger stays a component-level constant, matching `DbGlyph`'s own precedent.
- 2026-09-18 · **Geometry evolved past the LAW's own opening numbers during the build, on the maintainer's own further calls, and DESIGN rule 1 / AGENT-UX §16x record what actually shipped rather than the earlier draft.** 24px tools on a 28px pitch (not 28/32: seven slots at the wider pitch would not fit the 2×2 floor, rule 13), a GLASS pill body (`color-mix(var(--bg-raised) 72%) over backdrop-filter: blur(14px) saturate(1.25)`, `--radius-pill`) rather than an opaque `--radius-md` box, and CIRCULAR tools inside it (nested-corner arithmetic: 17 outer less a 5px inset is 12, half of a 24px tool); all three are the maintainer's own calls made on the sketch, written into §16x with the reasoning rather than asserted, and DESIGN rule 1 amended alongside so the token table matches the tree. The chord letter sits under the glyph at tier 2 (not tier 3 `--fg-faint`, which measured under-legible at 6px on a translucent surface in light theme) and the armed tool's letter takes the same glyph colour rather than an opacity mix (rule 3).
- 2026-09-18 · **The Pen ▾ menu retires; the cluster drops to six.** `blockTools.ts` DRAWING roster becomes `Grip · Undo · Redo · Copy · Ask · More`; `pen` leaves `BlockTool` and `select` (plus, beyond the LAW's own list, `eraser`, the maintainer's own ask for "added functionality" the move tool's own wave was already about) join `DrawTool`, read out of `DRAW_TOOLS` by lookup rather than position, `select` first as the island's own order names it.
- 2026-09-18 · **The select tool's math lives in one pure module, `src/canvas/select.ts`, ported from the sketch and then hardened past three bugs the sketch actually had.** Two frames only: a stroke's LOCAL box (what it was drawn in) and its WORLD box (that box at its own angle), with a hit test turning the pointer backward into the local frame rather than the shape forward. Every function is immutable and returns the input BY IDENTITY on a no-op (a zero move, a zero turn, a unit scale, a refused drag), which is what keeps an untouched stroke's memoised mark from re-rendering and makes `changed(a, b)` an identity walk instead of a deep compare. The fixed-corner resize is ONE path (always scale the local box about the fixed corner, then translate so that corner lands back in the world), not the sketch's separate branch for a rotated single shape, so a turned and an upright shape run identical code. Three sketch bugs closed here: a degenerate axis (a flat line, a pen dot) is HELD at a scale factor of 1 instead of divided by `|| 1`, which used to scale a tap to hundreds on its first drag; a text resize now holds its corner exactly via `labelResize`'s geometric-mean size rebuilt around that corner, where the sketch's `at`-scaling let the corner crawl; and one shared `textBox` metric (`strokes.ts`) now backs `bboxOf`, `localBox`, the rotation centre and the overlay alike, where the sketch measured selection and render off two different advances. Proven by an invariant test over 5 stroke kinds × 4 angles × 4 corners × 4 drags × shift on/off, and by the CDP probe's own knob/handle/marquee/undo sequence.
- 2026-09-18 · **Two more correctness points the sketch's JSON-clone approach could paper over and typed strokes cannot.** `r` (radians) is wrapped to `(-pi, pi]` and rounded to 4 places with the wrap boundary widened by that same rounding step, so a value sitting exactly at the edge does not flip sign on every read (LESSONS 1: the pair holds still). A rotated rectangle's floor padding is `t/2 * (|cos r| + |sin r|)`, not a flat `t/2`: a mitred corner reaches along both local axes, so `bboxOf` keeps its promise that the box never falls short of the ink at 45°.
- 2026-09-18 · **`r` splits the `Stroke` union rather than sitting on a field two kinds must remember to ignore.** rect/ellipse/text carry it; pen/line/arrow bake every transform into their points instead. `writeStrokes`/`parseStrokes` round-trip it (absent when 0, byte-identical for an untouched stroke); `marksOf`/`svgOf` emit `transform="rotate(deg cx cy)"`; `bboxOf` reads world boxes. A comment at both positive-only checks (`rect | ellipse`, not "not `line | arrow`") warns that the obvious refactor back is silently wrong, since TypeScript cannot narrow a union member by a negative check here.
- 2026-09-18 · **The overlay is one function serving both a hover and a selection, so nothing shifts under the finger at the moment of picking.** `overlayOf(strokes, indices)` answers a single stroke's own rotated box, a group's axis-aligned union box, four corner handles, a 16px stem and a 5px knob alike; the seat renders it at opacity .5 for a hover and full for a selection rather than drawing the two at different insets as the sketch did (3px vs 4px). The overlay itself is `pointer-events: none`; a press is tested against `handleAt`'s own math (knob → corner → hit → paper) so the rotated case stays honest without hit-testing the DOM.
- 2026-09-18 · **Gesture-level snapshots, not per-frame increments.** `moveSel`/`rotateSel`/`resizeSel` each take the snapshot the gesture started from and apply one delta, so a 300-frame drag is 300 pure applications of a single delta rather than 300 compounding roundings, every unselected stroke keeps its identity, and Drawing.tsx only dispatches gestures to the module rather than holding the one-vs-group branch itself. Every gesture commits ONCE on release (the element's own undo history); a marquee commits nothing; Esc during a drag drops to the snapshot.
- 2026-09-18 · **Integration seams, `Drawing.tsx`.** `prox.close()` opens every tool branch's `onDown` (text, select, full, ink alike), so the island is "closed within one frame of a pointerdown," probe-confirmed; `useIslandProximity(islandRef, islandOpen, setIslandOpen, () => inking)` owns the 40px reach, the 160ms close grace (re-checked against focus-within and the last `near` reading when the timer fires), opens on focusin, and never opens while a stroke is in flight; `close()` folds at once for a stroke's own pointerdown, no grace. Marks are cached per stroke identity in a `WeakMap` so a drag moving two of forty strokes rebuilds two marks, which is what makes select.ts's identity promise pay off in the render. A press on a tool never steals focus from the paper (`onMouseDown` preventDefault); Tab is the keyboard's own route in, and focus landing inside the island opens it.
- 2026-09-18 · **The keyboard reaches further than the LAW's own list, inside the island and on the sheet alike.** Arrow keys move focus across the six slots (opening the island), → opens a family's arm and focuses its visible member, ← at the first member folds the arm back onto its slot, Esc folds an open arm before it folds the island, and an unmodified chord typed while a tool is focused arms it; modifier chords bubble untouched (LESSONS 10). On the sheet: `v` arms select, ⌫/Delete deletes a standing selection and commits once, Esc clears the selection before it leaves the sheet, arrows nudge 1px (⇧ 10px) once per press, ⌘A selects all only with select armed, a text double-click opens the existing label editor in place, and Ink/Weight from the island restyle a standing selection rather than the block's own memory.
- 2026-09-18 · **A folded arm's visible member and its slot swap by z-order and a `data-handed` attribute, not by a class rename, and `data-open` is an attribute everywhere (rule 1 bans `.open` for a selection state).** An arm's own root spans its full open width unpainted so `nearIsland` can read the true open footprint off `getBoundingClientRect()` mid-spring; an arm is only `pointer-events: auto` and visible from the click that opens it to the instant its own fold spring completes (`onHome`), closing a first-frame bug where a folded island showed three raised boxes standing under it.
- 2026-09-18 · **Harness and probes.** `src/harness/fixtures.f3.ts` replaces the retired `fixtures.c2draw.ts` picker states (`c2-draw-tools`'s `.dw-pick` no longer exists) with eight states, all PRESSED through the product's own controls, not drawn: `f3-island-rest`, `f3-island-open`, `f3-island-arm`, `f3-island-floor` (the 2×2 floor, 201 inside 211, nothing clipped), `f3-select-one`, `f3-select-rotated`, `f3-select-multi`, `f3-select-marquee`, 54 frames at 640/960/1280 × both themes once the build's own `f3-erase-doomed` state (the eraser, beyond the LAW's list) is counted too. `docs/research/f3-frames/f3-probe.ts` (CDP, modelled on `f1-probe.ts`) samples the armed tool's translateY, every sibling's opacity and the body's height every 16ms for 600ms on open and on fold, and drives the select tool's full gesture set (hover, click, marquee, knob, handle, ⌫, ⌘Z ×4) against a real pointer: **186/186 PASS, 0 FAIL, all three widths** (`f3-probe.log`), crossing the slot once, overshoot capped at 8% of travel, settling inside 500ms, the body never clipping the armed tool on the way out or home, one undo step per gesture, a click on empty paper clearing the selection with no commit.
- 2026-09-18 · **This gate's own re-check against the shipped tree (LESSONS 9) found two non-blocking findings, both carried open rather than fixed this pass** (full text: `ROADMAP_log.md`'s F3 entry). **S2** (`drawing.css` `.dw-label`): the label editor's `<textarea>` has no `white-space: pre`, so a caption whose real glyph advance beats `textBox`'s 0.55em-per-char estimate can soft-wrap past `rows={1}` and clip the caret, while the committed stroke stays one `tspan`, new with the F3 textarea and not present when the editor was an `<input>`. **S3** (`docs/AGENT-UX.md` §16x): the sketch addendum's fold timing ("siblings fade in 60ms, body and armed tool wait 80ms") is not what shipped and §16x describes the replacement (the body's own gate, `--dur-quick`) without naming the substitution it made.
